Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in supersymmetry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 732 and the invariant I(A,B) = 602 mod 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by supersymmetry generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 602. QED.
